# Cosmetic Reward Catalog

*Generated 2026-10-08 by `ShardContent/tools/cosmetic-catalog/catalog.py build`. Edit the category files in `catalog/`, not this page.*

**1161 rewards in 29 categories.** Machine-readable copy: [`cosmetic-rewards.json`](cosmetic-rewards.json). Every reward has a permanent id `CR-<CAT>-<NNN>`; other systems should refer to rewards by that id and nothing else. Rewards that fall outside the era grading but are era neutral are in [`Era-Neutral-Reward-Catalog.md`](Era-Neutral-Reward-Catalog.md) (ids `CN-...`).

> **Felucca note.** Felucca runs the client's *Desolation* season, which hides every tile flagged *Foliage* (leaf canopies, many bushes). Entries that contain such tiles say so below and show an *as seen in Felucca* picture; the owner can change the map season if full leaves are wanted. The in-game gallery shots in `client-shots/` are taken in Felucca and show exactly what a player sees.
